131:def telemetry(bridge):
132:    """Marked copy of vis1_run.telemetry (VIS2-BATTERY-FIX): no 80 % upper bound; all other stops kept."""
133:    if (bridge/'abort').exists():
134:        raise SystemExit('owner aborted wrapper')
135:    d = json.loads((bridge/'latest.json').read_text())
136:    if time.monotonic() - d['t'] > 12:
137:        raise RuntimeError('native telemetry stale')
138:    if d.get('error'):
139:        raise RuntimeError('native telemetry failed: ' + d['error'])
140:    if d['capacity'] < 50 or d['battery_status'] != 'Discharging':
141:        raise RuntimeError('battery below 50% or not Discharging')
142:    if d['agent']:
143:        v1.log_agent_matches(d.get('agent_detail', []))
144:        raise RuntimeError('agent found')
145:    if d['skin'] is None or d['status'] is None:
146:        raise RuntimeError('missing VIRTUAL-SKIN or Android status')
147:    if d['status'] >= 4 or d['bat_c'] >= 45:
148:        raise RuntimeError('thermal stop (Android CRITICAL or battery >=45 C)')
149:    return d

152:def gate(bridge, idle):
153:    """Marked copy of vis1_run.gate using the local telemetry()."""
154:    start = time.monotonic()
155:    while True:
156:        d = telemetry(bridge)
157:        if v1.gate_ok(d['skin'], idle):
158:            return dict(skin=d['skin'], status=d['status'], waited_s=time.monotonic()-start, missed=False)
159:        if time.monotonic()-start >= 900:
160:            raise RuntimeError('gate missed: not cool after 15 minutes; no warm-start block')
161:        time.sleep(5)

163:
164:def guarded_post(bridge, pid, endpoint, body, timeout):
165:    """Request in a worker; telemetry/abort/affinity stay enforced every 0.5 s while it runs.
166:    A violation raises here and run_block's finally stops the owned server, ending the request."""
167:    result = {}
168:
169:    def work():
170:        try:
171:            result['data'] = v1.post(endpoint, body, timeout=timeout)
172:        except BaseException as e:
173:            result['error'] = e
174:    th = threading.Thread(target=work, daemon=True)
175:    th.start()
176:    while th.is_alive():
177:        th.join(.5)
178:        if th.is_alive():
179:            telemetry(bridge)
180:            v1.check_cores(pid)
181:    if 'error' in result:
182:        raise result['error']
183:    return result['data']

186:def sample_violations(samples):
187:    """Every recorded sample must satisfy the same stops telemetry() enforces live."""
188:    bad = []
189:    if any(s.get('agent') for s in samples):
190:        bad.append('agent')
191:    if any(s.get('battery_status') != 'Discharging' or s.get('capacity', -1) < 50 for s in samples):
192:        bad.append('battery band')
193:    if any(s.get('cores_ok') is False for s in samples):
194:        bad.append('affinity')
195:    if any((s.get('status') or 0) >= 4 or (s.get('bat_c') or 0) >= 45 for s in samples):
196:        bad.append('thermal stop threshold')
197:    return bad

200:def run_block(block, index, out, bridge, idle, seq, smoke):
201:    record = dict(phase_start=time.monotonic(), block=block, index=index, config=config_of(block),
202:                  label=SMOKE_LABEL if smoke else 'NOT VALID', reason='interrupted', requests=[],
203:                  command=command(block), started_epoch=time.time(), smoke=smoke)
204:    attempt = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ')
205:    path = out/f'{index:02d}_{block}_{attempt}.json'
206:    logpath = out/f'{index:02d}_{block}_{attempt}.server.log'
207:    proc = pid = None
208:    try:
209:        if not smoke:
210:            record['gate'] = gate(bridge, idle)
211:        v1.free_ports()
212:        if v1.agent_found(log=True):
213:            raise RuntimeError('agent found before launch')
214:        if not smoke:  # cold load: same native cache-drop handshake as VIS-1
215:            (bridge/'drop_done').unlink(missing_ok=True)
216:            (bridge/'drop_request').touch()
217:            began = time.monotonic()
218:            while not (bridge/'drop_done').exists():
219:                if time.monotonic()-began > 30:
220:                    raise RuntimeError('cache-drop handshake timeout')
221:                time.sleep(.1)
222:            if (bridge/'drop_done').read_text().strip() != 'ok':
223:                raise RuntimeError('cache drop failed')
224:            (bridge/'drop_request').unlink()
225:            (bridge/'drop_done').unlink()
226:            record['gate_after_drop'] = gate(bridge, idle)
227:        d = telemetry(bridge)
228:        record['battery_start'] = dict(capacity=d['capacity'], voltage_now_uV=d['voltage_now_uV'], t=d['t'])
229:        with logpath.open('w') as log:
230:            began = time.monotonic()
231:            proc = v1.spawn(record['command'], log)
232:            while pid is None:
233:                if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic()-began > 10:
234:                    raise RuntimeError('server crashed or owned child not found')
235:                try:
236:                    pid = v1.server_pid(proc)
237:                except (OSError, RuntimeError):
238:                    time.sleep(.05)
239:            v1.write(bridge/'target.json', dict(pid=pid))
240:            while True:
241:                if proc.poll() is not None:
242:                    raise RuntimeError('server crashed during load')
243:                try:
244:                    if v1.post('/health', timeout=1).get('status') == 'ok':
245:                        break
246:                except (OSError, ValueError):
247:                    pass
248:                telemetry(bridge)
249:                if time.monotonic()-began >= 180:
250:                    raise RuntimeError('server load timeout')
251:                time.sleep(.2)
252:            record.update(server_pid=pid, load_s=time.monotonic()-began, loaded_at=time.monotonic())
253:            v1.check_cores(pid)
254:            record['reported'] = verify_config(block)
255:            record['pss_after_load'] = fresh_pss(bridge, pid)
256:            warm = next(x for x in seq if x[0] == 'gen')
257:            guarded_post(bridge, pid, warm[2], warm[3], REQ_TIMEOUT_S)  # discarded warm-up (generation)
258:            record['measure_start'] = start = time.monotonic()
259:            for kind, name, endpoint, body in seq:
260:                telemetry(bridge)
261:                v1.check_cores(pid)
262:                left = BLOCK_CAP_S - (time.monotonic()-start)
263:                if left <= 0:
264:                    raise RuntimeError(f'block cap {BLOCK_CAP_S} s exceeded')
265:                if kind == 'image':
266:                    record['pss_before_image'] = fresh_pss(bridge, pid)
267:                offset = logpath.stat().st_size
268:                t = time.monotonic()
269:                data = guarded_post(bridge, pid, endpoint, body, min(REQ_TIMEOUT_S, left))
270:                end = time.monotonic()
271:                if end-start > BLOCK_CAP_S:
272:                    raise RuntimeError(f'block cap {BLOCK_CAP_S} s exceeded')
273:                if kind == 'image':
274:                    record['pss_after_image'] = fresh_pss(bridge, pid)
275:                with logpath.open() as f:
276:                    f.seek(offset)
277:                    delta = f.read()
278:                row = v1.request_record(name, data, t, end, delta, 'V2' if kind == 'image' else 'T2')
279:                row.update(kind=kind, cache_n=row['timings'].get('cache_n'),
280:                           prompt_per_second=row['timings'].get('prompt_per_second'))
281:                record['requests'].append(row)
282:                v1.write(path, record)
283:            record['measure_end'] = time.monotonic()
284:            if proc.poll() is not None:
285:                raise RuntimeError('server crashed during measurement')
286:            v1.check_cores(pid)
287:            telemetry(bridge)
288:            record.update(label=SMOKE_LABEL if smoke else 'VALID', reason='completed fixed request sequence')
289:    except (Exception, SystemExit) as e:
290:        record.update(label='NOT VALID', reason=str(e))
291:    finally:
292:        record['stop_start'] = time.monotonic()
293:        v1.write(bridge/'target.json', dict(pid=None))
294:        if proc is not None:
295:            record['server_exit'] = v1.own_stop(proc, pid)
296:            record['exit_clean'] = record['server_exit'] == 0 and not v1.group_alive(proc.pid)
297:        record['stopped'] = time.monotonic()
298:        record['samples'] = [s for s in v1.rows(bridge/'samples.jsonl')
299:                             if record['phase_start']-1 <= s['t'] <= record['stopped']]
300:        record['lmk'] = v1.lmk_request(bridge, record['started_epoch'])
301:        record['metrics'] = v1.metrics(record)
302:        if record['label'] != 'NOT VALID':  # VALID and SMOKE both need a clean finish
303:            bad = []
304:            if proc is not None and not record['exit_clean']:
305:                bad.append(f"server cleanup not clean (exit {record['server_exit']})")
306:            if not record['lmk'].get('ok'):
307:                bad.append('LMK query failed')
308:            if sample_violations(record['samples']):
309:                bad.append('sample violation: ' + ', '.join(sample_violations(record['samples'])))
310:            if [e for e in v1.rows(bridge/'errors.jsonl') if record['phase_start'] <= e['t'] <= record['stop_start']]:
311:                bad.append('sampling failed')
312:            if bad:
313:                record.update(label='NOT VALID', reason='; '.join(bad))
314:        v1.write(path, record)
315:    return record

320:    if not sel or any(b not in BLOCKS for b in sel):
321:        raise ValueError('--blocks takes a comma list of A,B,C,D,M (repeats allowed, e.g. A,B,B,A)')
322:    return sel

325:def code_paths():
326:    return ['benchmark/gemma_vision/vis2/vis2_run.py', 'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis2/oneshot.sh',
327:            'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis1/vis1_run.py', 'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis0/vis0.py',

332:def manifest(selected, smoke, gen, unc, cached, image):
333:    allp = gen + [unc] + cached + ([(IMAGE_FRAME, image)] if image else [])
334:    return dict(blocks=selected, smoke=smoke, rounds=ROUNDS, gen_n=GEN_N, block_cap_s=BLOCK_CAP_S,
335:                commands={b: command(b) for b in sorted(set(selected))},
336:                payload_sha256={n: hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(p, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest() for n, p in allp},
337:                code_sha256={p: v1.sha(REPO/p) for p in code_paths()},
338:                weight_sha256={str(p): v1.sha(p) for p in WEIGHTS},
339:                binary_sha256={str(p): v1.sha(p) for p in [BIN] + LIBS},
340:                base_commit=subprocess.check_output(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd=REPO, text=True).strip(),
341:                git_status=subprocess.check_output(['git', 'status', '--porcelain=v1'], cwd=REPO, text=True))

343:
344:def preflight():
345:    """Before and after the 5-minute idle: ports, agents, files/hashes, plus Debian imports or native root/battery/cpuset."""
346:    v1.free_ports()
347:    matches = v1.agent_matches()
348:    if matches:
349:        v1.log_agent_matches(matches)
350:        raise SystemExit('agent found; close Claude/AGY/Codex (see pid/comm/cmdline above)')
351:    missing = [str(p) for p in (*WEIGHTS, BIN) if not p.is_file()]
352:    if missing or not LIBS:
353:        raise SystemExit('missing file(s): ' + ', '.join(missing or ['llama.cpp-vis shared libraries']))
354:    bad = [str(p) for p, h in WEIGHTS.items() if v1.sha(p) != h]
355:    if bad:
356:        raise SystemExit('SHA-256 mismatch: ' + ', '.join(bad))
357:    if Path('/termux-home').is_dir():
358:        missing_mods = [m for m in v1.DEBIAN_IMPORTS if importlib.util.find_spec(m) is None]
359:        if missing_mods:
360:            raise SystemExit('Debian python3 is missing ' + ', '.join(missing_mods))
361:    else:
362:        if subprocess.run(['su', '-c', 'id'], capture_output=True, timeout=10).returncode != 0:
363:            raise SystemExit('no root (su) access; telemetry needs root')
364:        cap, status = v1.root(f'cat {v1.BAT}/capacity {v1.BAT}/status', 'preflight_battery').split()[:2]
365:        if not (cap.isdigit() and int(cap) >= 50 and status == 'Discharging'):
366:            raise SystemExit('battery below 50% or not Discharging; unplug the charger')
367:        before = os.sched_getaffinity(0)
368:        os.sched_setaffinity(0, {4, 5, 6, 7})
369:        stuck = os.sched_getaffinity(0) == {4, 5, 6, 7}
370:        os.sched_setaffinity(0, before)
371:        if not stuck:
372:            raise SystemExit('Termux cpuset blocks cores 4-7; bring Termux to the foreground, screen on')
373:    print('PREFLIGHT OK', flush=True)
374:
375:
376:def mib(snap):
377:    return snap['pss_kib'] and round(snap['pss_kib']/1024) if snap else None
378:
379:
380:def med(xs):
381:    xs = [x for x in xs if x is not None]
382:    return round(statistics.median(xs), 2) if xs else None
383:
384:
385:def summarize(folder):
386:    """Per-block medians from saved records only; missing fields print as None (unavailable)."""
387:    lines = []
388:    for p in sorted(Path(folder).glob('[0-9][0-9]_[A-DM]_*Z.json')):
389:        d = json.loads(p.read_text())
390:        r, m = d['requests'], d.get('metrics', {})
391:        by = lambda k: [x for x in r if x['kind'] == k]
392:        dn = sum(x.get('draft_n') or 0 for x in by('gen'))
393:        da = sum(x.get('draft_n_accepted') or 0 for x in by('gen'))
394:        lines.append(dict(
395:            block=f"{d['index']:02d}_{d['block']}", label=d['label'], reason=d['reason'], load_s=med([d.get('load_s')]),
396:            battery_start=d.get('battery_start'),
397:            reported=d.get('reported'), uncached_pp_tok_s=med([x['prompt_per_second'] for x in by('uncached')]),
398:            uncached_prompt_n=sorted({x['prompt_tokens'] for x in by('uncached')}),
399:            cached_ms=med([x['prompt_ms'] for x in by('cached')]),
400:            cached_cache_n=sorted({x['cache_n'] for x in by('cached')}),
401:            gen_tok_s=med([x['predicted_per_second'] for x in by('gen')]),
402:            gen_tokens=[x['generated_tokens'] for x in by('gen')],
403:            gen_wall_ms=med([x['wall_ms'] for x in by('gen')]),
404:            draft_acceptance=round(da/dn, 3) if dn else None,
405:            image=[dict(wall_ms=round(x['wall_ms']), prompt_tokens=x['prompt_tokens'], usage=x.get('usage'))
406:                   for x in by('image')],
407:            pss_after_load_mib=mib(d.get('pss_after_load')),
408:            peak_pss_mib=m.get('peak_pss_kib') and round(m['peak_pss_kib']/1024),
409:            pss_image_before_after_mib=[mib(d.get('pss_before_image')), mib(d.get('pss_after_image'))],
410:            checks=dict(uncached_cache_n_zero=all(x['cache_n'] == 0 for x in by('uncached')),
411:                        cached_reuse=bool(by('cached')) and all((x['cache_n'] or 0) > 0 for x in by('cached')),
412:                        replies_nonempty=all((x.get('reply') or '').strip() for x in r)),
413:            min_mem_available_mib=m.get('min_mem_available_kib') and round(m['min_mem_available_kib']/1024),
414:            lmk=d.get('lmk'), server_exit=d.get('server_exit'), exit_clean=d.get('exit_clean'),
415:            skin=[m.get('skin_start'), m.get('skin_end')], status_max=m.get('status_max'),
416:            caps={k: round(v['percent']) for k, v in m.get('caps', {}).items()}, power=m.get('power')))
417:    for line in lines:
418:        print(json.dumps(line), flush=True)
419:    # VALID covers execution conditions only; these say whether blocks are comparable at all.
420:    timed = [x for x in lines if not x['block'].endswith('M') and x['label'] == 'VALID']
421:    print(json.dumps(dict(comparability=dict(
422:        gen_tokens_identical=len({tuple(x['gen_tokens']) for x in timed}) <= 1,
423:        uncached_prompt_n_identical=len({tuple(x['uncached_prompt_n']) for x in timed}) <= 1,
424:        all_checks_pass=all(all(x['checks'].values()) for x in lines)))), flush=True)
425:    return lines
426:
427:
428:def main(argv=None):
429:    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
430:    ap.add_argument('--blocks', default='A,B,B,A')
431:    ap.add_argument('--smoke', action='store_true')
432:    ap.add_argument('--dry-run', action='store_true')
433:    ap.add_argument('--summarize', type=Path)
434:    ap.add_argument('--bridge', type=Path)
435:    ap.add_argument('--preflight', action='store_true', help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
436:    ap.add_argument('--monitor', type=Path, help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
437:    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
438:    if a.preflight:
439:        return preflight()
440:    if a.monitor:
441:        return v1.monitor(a.monitor)
442:    if a.summarize:
443:        return summarize(a.summarize)
444:    selected = blocks(a.blocks)
445:    if a.dry_run:
446:        gen, unc, cached = payloads()
447:        print(DRY_LABEL)
448:        print(json.dumps(dict(blocks=selected, commands={b: command(b) for b in sorted(set(selected))},
449:                              sequence=[(b, [(k, n, e) for k, n, e, _ in sequence(b, a.smoke, gen, unc, cached, {})])
450:                                        for b in sorted(set(selected))],
451:                              uncached_chars=len(unc[1]['prompt'])), indent=1))
452:        return
453:    if a.bridge is None or not Path('/termux-home').is_dir():
454:        raise SystemExit('launch only through native vis2/oneshot.sh into Debian')
455:    out = bridge = a.bridge.resolve()
456:    if not (bridge/'owner_launch').is_file():
457:        raise SystemExit('missing owner wrapper receipt')
458:    if v1.agent_found(log=True):
459:        raise SystemExit('agent found; no server started')
460:    v1.free_ports()
461:    telemetry(bridge)
462:    os.sched_setaffinity(0, {4, 5, 6, 7})
463:    v1.check_cores(os.getpid())
464:    for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP):
465:        signal.signal(sig, v1.interrupted)
466:    gen, unc, cached = payloads()
467:    image = image_payload() if 'M' in selected else None
468:    if any(v1.sha(p) != h for p, h in WEIGHTS.items()):
469:        raise SystemExit('model/projector/drafter SHA-256 mismatch')
470:    idle = None if a.smoke else json.loads((bridge/'idle.json').read_text())['skin']
471:    v1.write(out/'run.json', dict(manifest=manifest(selected, a.smoke, gen, unc, cached, image), idle_skin=idle,
472:             note='Short blocks; thermal equivalence between blocks is not established (#93). '
473:                  + (SMOKE_LABEL if a.smoke else 'ABBA order controls linear drift only.')))
474:    print('RUN_FOLDER='+str(out), flush=True)
475:    for i, b in enumerate(selected):
476:        result = run_block(b, i, out, bridge, idle, sequence(b, a.smoke, gen, unc, cached, image), a.smoke)
477:        print(f"{i:02d} {b}: {result['label']} — {result['reason']}", flush=True)
478:        if result['label'] == 'NOT VALID':
479:            raise SystemExit('stopped; later blocks not run. See README recovery.')
480:    summarize(out)
481:    print('Finished. Package RUN_FOLDER (see README); do not rerun for analysis.', flush=True)
482:
483:
484:if __name__ == '__main__':
485:    try:
486:        main()
487:    finally:
488:        for proc in v1.OWNED:
489:            v1.own_stop(proc)

benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:7:## Why VIS-3 (diagnosis, 2026-10-22, read-only)
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:8:
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:9:The builds are equivalent: same Clang 21.1.8, `-O3`, `-march=armv8.2-a+dotprod+fp16`, OpenMP, REPACK, LLAMAFILE,
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:10:shared libraries, no KleidiAI. Both have `sdot` (1045 vs 1117) and the same 456 FP16 `fmla .8h`. The CMake caches
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:11:differ only in `LLAMA_BUILD_IS_DEV` (version string) and `LLAMA_BUILD_UI`. The runtime defaults are also the same on both:
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:12:mmap loading, flash attention `auto` (enabled), batch 2048, ubatch 512, `--fit on`, the MTP draft inheriting the
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:13:target's threads, `--swa-full` and `--cache-ram 0` honoured. b11716 creates an explicit ggml threadpool. Under OpenMP this
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:14:is plausibly neutral, but that is unverified.
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:15:
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:16:The earlier OLD vs NEW numbers come from different thermal conditions. The VIS gate is idle + 1.5 °C, and session idles were
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:17:27–36.7 °C. VIS-1 T1 (b1609 + MTP) started at skin 39 °C with the X1 capped at 1.58 GHz and ran at 7.07 gen tok/s. VIS-1 T2
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:18:(b11716 + MTP) started at 32.5 °C and ran at 9.22. VIS-2 A (b11716 + MTP) started at 34.2 °C and ran at 7.74. The #137
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:19:chat_mtp run (b1609 + MTP) started at about 24 °C and ran at 19.18. These runs also used different prompts and sampling. None of them
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:20:isolates the build.
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:21:
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:22:## Design
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:23:
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:24:| Block | Binary | MTP drafter |
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:25:|---|---|---|
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:26:| O | `~/llama.cpp-b1609-dotprod` (robot) | off |
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:27:| N | `~/llama.cpp-vis` (b11716) | off |
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:28:| OM / NM (optional) | as O / N | `--model-draft … --spec-type draft-mtp --spec-draft-n-max 3` |
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:29:
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:30:Command = `server_manager.start_setup('setup_q4')` (or `setup_q4_mtp`) exactly, with `taskset -c 4-7`, `timeout 900`
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:31:and port 8091. A test checks this against `server_manager.py`. OLD and NEW differ only in the binary path. No projector
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:32:and no `--reasoning` flag: `/completion` with the raw chat.py prompt never uses the chat template.
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:33:
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:34:Per block: the strengthened gate (skin ≤ idle + 1.5 °C **and** ≤ `SKIN_START_MAX` = 32.0 °C, waiting up to 30 min), then a
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:35:cache drop and the gate again. **Cold phase:** launch, readiness, `/props` and `/slots` checks (no vision, intended MTP,
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:36:n_ctx 2048), the mapped libraries in `/proc/<pid>/maps` must come from the block's own build, PSS, then the first response
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:37:(gen[0] prompt, 16 tokens), then a clean stop. **Warm phase:** immediate relaunch (files still cached), the same checks
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:38:and first response, then 4 rounds of U (≈1005-token uncached log, 8 tokens), K (cached prefix, 8 tokens) and G (VIS-1
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:39:prompt, greedy, up to 128 tokens), then a clean stop. Requests are greedy (temperature 0, top_k 1, seed 0) and identical
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:40:for every block.
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:41:
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:42:Recorded: cold and warm load time (spawn to `/health` ok) plus log-phase timestamps, first-response wall time, prompt
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:43:and generation tok/s, cache reuse, draft acceptance, and the mean CPU frequency per request from cpufreq `time_in_state`
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:44:(policy4/policy6, no sampling gap). Also recorded: PSS after load and at the end, peak PSS, minimum MemAvailable, battery W,
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:45:skin, Android status, CPU caps, LMK lines, and exit code and stop time.
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:46:
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:47:`VALID` means only that execution conditions held. `--summarize` prints per-config medians, NEW/OLD ratios and the
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:48:comparability checks: both builds present, balanced, identical prompt token counts (tokenization), identical generated
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:49:lengths, start-skin spread, and every check passing. Draw conclusions only after that assessment.

benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:59:
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:60:Expected time for `O,N,N,O`: 5 min idle, then about 4 min active per block (cold + warm load about 25 s, 4 rounds about
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:61:170 s), plus cooling to the gate between blocks (typically 5–15 min each). That is about 35–75 min in total. If the phone
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:62:idles above 32 °C the gate cannot pass: cool the room or remove the case. Never raise the ceiling just to make a run pass.
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:63:
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:64:Each launch writes one folder, `vis3/runs/<UTC>/` (or `smoke_<UTC>/`). It holds `run.json` (commands, code, payload,
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:65:weight and binary/library hashes, base commit, git status), one JSON per block, `.cold.server.log` / `.warm.server.log`,
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:66:`samples.jsonl` and `console.log`. A failed block stops the launch and the earlier blocks stay. Rerun only the missing blocks, as a
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:67:new folder (e.g. `--blocks N,O`). The summary pools folders and refuses if their code, payloads, weights, binaries or
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:68:constants differ:

benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:10:shared libraries, no KleidiAI. Both have `sdot` (1045 vs 1117) and the same 456 FP16 `fmla .8h`. The CMake caches
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:11:differ only in `LLAMA_BUILD_IS_DEV` (version string) and `LLAMA_BUILD_UI`. The runtime defaults are also the same on both:
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:12:mmap loading, flash attention `auto` (enabled), batch 2048, ubatch 512, `--fit on`, the MTP draft inheriting the
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:13:target's threads, `--swa-full` and `--cache-ram 0` honoured. b11716 creates an explicit ggml threadpool. Under OpenMP this
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:14:is plausibly neutral, but that is unverified.

benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:33:
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:34:Per block: the strengthened gate (skin ≤ idle + 1.5 °C **and** ≤ `SKIN_START_MAX` = 32.0 °C, waiting up to 30 min), then a
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:35:cache drop and the gate again. **Cold phase:** launch, readiness, `/props` and `/slots` checks (no vision, intended MTP,
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:36:n_ctx 2048), the mapped libraries in `/proc/<pid>/maps` must come from the block's own build, PSS, then the first response
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:37:(gen[0] prompt, 16 tokens), then a clean stop. **Warm phase:** immediate relaunch (files still cached), the same checks
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:38:and first response, then 4 rounds of U (≈1005-token uncached log, 8 tokens), K (cached prefix, 8 tokens) and G (VIS-1
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:39:prompt, greedy, up to 128 tokens), then a clean stop. Requests are greedy (temperature 0, top_k 1, seed 0) and identical
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:40:for every block.
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:41:
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:42:Recorded: cold and warm load time (spawn to `/health` ok) plus log-phase timestamps, first-response wall time, prompt
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:43:and generation tok/s, cache reuse, draft acceptance, and the mean CPU frequency per request from cpufreq `time_in_state`
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:44:(policy4/policy6, no sampling gap). Also recorded: PSS after load and at the end, peak PSS, minimum MemAvailable, battery W,
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:45:skin, Android status, CPU caps, LMK lines, and exit code and stop time.
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:46:

benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:49:lengths, start-skin spread, and every check passing. Draw conclusions only after that assessment.

benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:61:170 s), plus cooling to the gate between blocks (typically 5–15 min each). That is about 35–75 min in total. If the phone
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:62:idles above 32 °C the gate cannot pass: cool the room or remove the case. Never raise the ceiling just to make a run pass.

benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:66:`samples.jsonl` and `console.log`. A failed block stops the launch and the earlier blocks stay. Rerun only the missing blocks, as a
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:67:new folder (e.g. `--blocks N,O`). The summary pools folders and refuses if their code, payloads, weights, binaries or

158:def own_stop(proc, server=None):
159:    """Only the private process group created by our Popen; timeout is a second hard bound."""
160:    if proc.poll() is None:
161:        # VIS1-FIX3: GNU timeout re-sends a TERM it receives to its whole group (kill(0, sig)), so
162:        # TERM to timeout reached llama-server twice ("second interrupt" -> abort). TERM the owned
163:        # server once (still our direct child in our group); timeout then exits with its status.
164:        # Server not known yet: fall back to timeout.
165:        owned = server is not None and (server, proc.pid, proc.pid) in set(proc_rows())
166:        try:
167:            os.kill(server if owned else proc.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
168:            proc.wait(timeout=3)
169:        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
170:            os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
171:            proc.wait(timeout=5)
172:        except ProcessLookupError:
173:            proc.wait(timeout=5)
174:    return proc.returncode
177:OWNED = []
178:DEFERRED = dict(on=False, signum=None)
181:def interrupted(n, frame):
182:    if DEFERRED['on']:
183:        DEFERRED['signum'] = n
184:    else:
185:        raise SystemExit(f'interrupted {n}')
188:def spawn(cmd, log):
189:    DEFERRED.update(on=True, signum=None)
190:    try:
191:        proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
192:                                start_new_session=True)
193:        OWNED.append(proc)
194:    finally:
195:        DEFERRED['on'] = False
196:    if DEFERRED['signum'] is not None:
197:        own_stop(proc)
198:        raise SystemExit(f'interrupted {DEFERRED["signum"]}')
199:    return proc
202:AGENT_NAMES = {'claude', 'codex', 'agy'}
205:def agent_matches():
206:    """Processes whose own binary is claude/codex/agy, not merely a path or argument containing those
207:    letters (VIS1-FIX: pgrep -f alone matches substrings anywhere in argv, e.g. a --config path under
208:    ~/.codex/). pgrep -f still casts the wide net; comm/argv0 narrows it to real agent processes,
209:    including a lingering app-server-daemon left behind after the owner closes the interactive CLI."""
210:    p = subprocess.run(['pgrep', '-f', 'claude|codex|agy'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
211:    if p.returncode not in (0, 1):
212:        raise RuntimeError('agent process check failed')
213:    found = []
214:    for token in p.stdout.split():
215:        pid = int(token)
216:        if pid == os.getpid():
217:            continue
218:        try:
219:            comm = Path(f'/proc/{pid}/comm').read_text().strip()
220:            parts = Path(f'/proc/{pid}/cmdline').read_bytes().split(b'\0')
221:            cmdline = ' '.join(c.decode(errors='replace') for c in parts if c)
222:        except (FileNotFoundError, ProcessLookupError):
223:            continue  # exited between pgrep and read
224:        argv0 = Path(cmdline.split(' ', 1)[0]).name if cmdline else ''
225:        if comm in AGENT_NAMES or argv0 in AGENT_NAMES:
226:            found.append(dict(pid=pid, comm=comm, cmdline=cmdline))
227:    return found
230:def log_agent_matches(matches):
231:    for m in matches:
232:        print(f"agent match: pid={m['pid']} comm={m['comm']} cmdline={m['cmdline']}", flush=True)
235:def agent_found(log=False):
236:    matches = agent_matches()
237:    if matches and log:
238:        log_agent_matches(matches)
239:    return bool(matches)
242:def free_ports():
243:    if os.environ.get('PREFIX') == '/data/data/com.termux/files/usr' and not Path('/termux-home').is_dir():
244:        listeners = root('/system/bin/ss -H -ltn','ports')
245:        for line in listeners.splitlines():
246:            fields = line.split()
247:            if len(fields) >= 4 and re.search(r':(?:8080|8090|8091)$',fields[3]):
248:                raise RuntimeError('existing listener on a VIS-1 reserved port')
249:    for port in (8080, 8090, 8091):
250:        # Check IPv4 and IPv6 loopback, including non-HTTP listeners.
251:        for family, host in ((socket.AF_INET, '127.0.0.1'), (socket.AF_INET6, '::1')):
252:            with socket.socket(family) as s:
253:                s.settimeout(.3)
254:                if s.connect_ex((host, port)) == 0:
255:                    raise RuntimeError(f'port {port} already has a listener')
258:def gate_ok(skin, idle):
259:    return skin is not None and math.isfinite(skin) and skin <= idle + 1.5
262:def telemetry(bridge, allow_agent=False):
263:    if (bridge/'abort').exists():
264:        raise SystemExit('owner aborted wrapper')
265:    d = json.loads((bridge/'latest.json').read_text())
266:    if time.monotonic() - d['t'] > 12:
267:        raise RuntimeError('native telemetry stale')
268:    if d.get('error'):
269:        raise RuntimeError('native telemetry failed: ' + d['error'])
270:    if not 50 <= d['capacity'] <= 80 or d['battery_status'] != 'Discharging':
271:        raise RuntimeError('battery outside 50-80% Discharging band')
272:    if d['agent'] and not allow_agent:
273:        log_agent_matches(d.get('agent_detail', []))
274:        raise RuntimeError('agent found')
275:    if d['skin'] is None or d['status'] is None:
276:        raise RuntimeError('missing VIRTUAL-SKIN or Android status')
277:    if d['status'] >= 4 or d['bat_c'] >= 45:
278:        raise RuntimeError('thermal stop (Android CRITICAL or battery >=45 C)')
279:    return d
282:def gate(bridge, idle, now=time.monotonic, sleep=time.sleep):
283:    start = now()
284:    while True:
285:        d = telemetry(bridge)
286:        if gate_ok(d['skin'], idle):
287:            return dict(skin=d['skin'], status=d['status'], waited_s=now()-start, missed=False)
288:        if now()-start >= 900:
289:            raise RuntimeError('gate missed: not cool after 15 minutes; no warm-start block')
290:        sleep(5)
293:def post(path, body=None, timeout=30):
294:    req = urllib.request.Request('http://127.0.0.1:8091'+path,
295:        data=None if body is None else json.dumps(body).encode(),
296:        headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
297:    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r:
298:        return json.load(r)
301:def proc_rows():
302:    """(pid, ppid, pgid) for every process, from /proc/<n>/stat (comm may contain spaces/parens)."""
303:    for d in Path('/proc').iterdir():
304:        if d.name.isdigit():
305:            try:
306:                rest = (d/'stat').read_text().rsplit(')', 1)[1].split()
307:                yield int(d.name), int(rest[1]), int(rest[2])
308:            except (OSError, IndexError, ValueError):
309:                pass  # exited mid-scan
312:def server_pid(proc):
313:    # VIS1-FIX2: /proc/<pid>/task/<pid>/children does not exist on this kernel, so every block
314:    # timed out here and killed a healthy server. Same rule as before, via ppid/pgid, never by name.
315:    children = [pid for pid, ppid, pgid in proc_rows() if ppid == proc.pid and pgid == proc.pid]
316:    if len(children) != 1:
317:        raise RuntimeError('cannot identify the owned timeout child')
318:    return children[0]
321:def group_alive(pgid):
322:    return any(g == pgid for _, _, g in proc_rows())
325:def check_cores(pid):
326:    # Also check the Android cpuset: affinity alone can hide a background restriction.
327:    status = Path(f'/proc/{pid}/status').read_text()
328:    m = re.search(r'^Cpus_allowed_list:\s*(.+)$', status, re.M)
329:    if not m or m.group(1).strip() != '4-7':
330:        raise RuntimeError('Termux/server moved off cores 4-7')
331:    # Each thread inherits the launch mask; inspect every server thread for later changes.
332:    for p in Path(f'/proc/{pid}/task').glob('*/status'):
333:        match = re.search(r'^Cpus_allowed_list:\s*(.+)$', p.read_text(), re.M)
334:        if not match or match.group(1).strip() != '4-7':
335:            raise RuntimeError('server thread moved off cores 4-7')
338:VIS0_PARSER = None
340:def request_record(name, data, start, end, log_delta, block):
341:    tm = data.get('timings', {})
342:    if not tm:
343:        raise RuntimeError('response missing server timings')
344:    reply = data.get('content')
345:    if block.startswith('V'):
346:        reply = data['choices'][0]['message']['content']
347:    # VIS-0 log parser has no server side effects. Rename its agent-era field explicitly.
348:    global VIS0_PARSER
349:    if block.startswith('V') and VIS0_PARSER is None:
350:        VIS0_PARSER = module('vis1_vis0', 'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis0/vis0.py').parse_server_log_delta
351:    parsed = VIS0_PARSER(log_delta) if block.startswith('V') else {}
352:    return dict(input=name, started=start, ended=end, wall_ms=(end-start)*1000, reply=reply,
353:                timings=tm, prompt_tokens=tm.get('prompt_n'), prompt_ms=tm.get('prompt_ms'),
354:                generated_tokens=tm.get('predicted_n'), predicted_per_second=tm.get('predicted_per_second'),
355:                draft_n=tm.get('draft_n'), draft_n_accepted=tm.get('draft_n_accepted'),
356:                image_encode_ms=parsed.get('image_encode_ms_not_a_measurement'),
357:                image_tokens=parsed.get('image_tokens_logged'), usage=data.get('usage'),
358:                budget=None if block.startswith('T') else (70 if block in ('V1','V2') else 140),
359:                mmproj=None if block.startswith('T') else ('BF16' if block in ('V1','V4') else 'Q8_0'))
362:def run_block(block, out, bridge, idle, payloads, t2_draft):
363:    record = dict(phase_start=time.monotonic(), block=block, label='NOT VALID', reason='interrupted', requests=[], samples=[],
364:                  gate=None, command=command(block,t2_draft), started_epoch=time.time())
365:    attempt = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ')
366:    path = out/f'{block}_{attempt}.json'
367:    logpath = out/f'{block}_{attempt}.server.log'
368:    proc = pid = None
369:    try:
370:        record['gate'] = gate(bridge,idle)
371:        free_ports()
372:        if agent_found(log=True):
373:            raise RuntimeError('agent found before launch')
374:        # Cold load handshake, served only by native owner wrapper.
375:        (bridge/'drop_done').unlink(missing_ok=True)
376:        (bridge/'drop_request').touch()
377:        began = time.monotonic()
378:        while not (bridge/'drop_done').exists():
379:            if time.monotonic()-began > 30:
380:                raise RuntimeError('cache-drop handshake timeout')
381:            time.sleep(.1)
382:        if (bridge/'drop_done').read_text().strip() != 'ok':
383:            raise RuntimeError('cache drop failed')
384:        (bridge/'drop_request').unlink()
385:        (bridge/'drop_done').unlink()
386:        # Cache drop can take time: the actual launch still must satisfy the gate.
387:        record['gate_after_drop'] = gate(bridge,idle)
388:        with logpath.open('w') as log:
389:            began = time.monotonic()
390:            proc = spawn(record['command'],log)
391:            pid = None
392:            while pid is None:
393:                if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic()-began > 10:
394:                    raise RuntimeError('server crashed or owned child not found')
395:                try:
396:                    pid = server_pid(proc)
397:                except (OSError,RuntimeError):
398:                    time.sleep(.05)
399:            write(bridge/'target.json',dict(pid=pid))
400:            while True:
401:                if proc.poll() is not None:
402:                    raise RuntimeError('server crashed during load')
403:                try:
404:                    if post('/health',timeout=1).get('status') == 'ok':
405:                        break
406:                except (OSError, ValueError):
407:                    pass
408:                telemetry(bridge)
409:                if time.monotonic()-began >= 180:
410:                    raise RuntimeError('server load timeout')
411:                time.sleep(.2)
412:            pid = server_pid(proc)
413:            write(bridge/'target.json',dict(pid=pid))
414:            record.update(server_pid=pid,load_s=time.monotonic()-began)
415:            check_cores(pid)
416:            endpoint = '/completion' if block.startswith('T') else '/v1/chat/completions'
417:            post(endpoint,payloads[0][1],timeout=180)  # discarded warm-up
418:            check_cores(pid)
419:            telemetry(bridge)
420:            record['measure_start'] = start = time.monotonic()
421:            deadline = start+BLOCK_S
422:            k = 0
423:            while time.monotonic() < deadline:
424:                telemetry(bridge)
425:                check_cores(pid)
426:                name, body = payloads[k % len(payloads)]
427:                offset = logpath.stat().st_size
428:                t = time.monotonic()
429:                try:
430:                    data = post(endpoint,body,timeout=max(.001,deadline-t))
431:                except (TimeoutError, OSError) as e:
432:                    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
433:                        record['cutoff_request'] = dict(input=name,reason='180 s boundary; excluded',error=type(e).__name__)
434:                        break
435:                    raise
436:                end = time.monotonic()
437:                if end > deadline:
438:                    record['cutoff_request'] = dict(input=name,reason='reply after boundary; excluded')
439:                    break
440:                with logpath.open() as f:
441:                    f.seek(offset)
442:                    delta = f.read()
443:                row = request_record(name,data,t,end,delta,block)
444:                record['requests'].append(row)
445:                write(path,record)  # keep replies across interruption
446:                k += 1
447:            record['measure_end'] = deadline
448:            if proc.poll() is not None:
449:                raise RuntimeError('server crashed during measurement')
450:            check_cores(pid)
451:            telemetry(bridge)
452:            record.update(label='VALID',reason='completed 180 s request window')
453:    except (Exception, SystemExit) as e:
454:        record.update(label='NOT VALID',reason=str(e))
455:    finally:
456:        record['stop_start'] = time.monotonic()
457:        write(bridge/'target.json',dict(pid=None))
458:        if proc is not None:
459:            record['server_exit'] = own_stop(proc, pid)
460:        record['stopped'] = time.monotonic()
461:        record['samples'] = [s for s in rows(bridge/'samples.jsonl')
462:                             if s['t'] >= record['phase_start']-1
463:                             and s['t'] <= record['stopped']]
464:        record['lmk'] = lmk_request(bridge,record['started_epoch'])
465:        if not record['lmk'].get('ok'):
466:            record.update(label='NOT VALID',reason=record['reason']+'; LMK query failed')
467:        if not record['requests']:
468:            record.update(label='NOT VALID',reason=record['reason']+'; no completed requests')
469:        record['metrics'] = metrics(record)
470:        if any(s.get('agent') or s.get('battery_status') != 'Discharging' or not 50 <= s.get('capacity',-1) <= 80
471:               or s.get('cores_ok') is False for s in record['samples']):
472:            record.update(label='NOT VALID',reason=record['reason']+'; agent, battery or affinity violation in samples')
473:        errors = [e for e in rows(bridge/'errors.jsonl') if record['phase_start'] <= e['t'] <= record['stop_start']]
474:        record['sampling_errors'] = errors
475:        if errors:
476:            record.update(label='NOT VALID',reason=record['reason']+'; sampling failed')
477:        write(path,record)
478:    return record
481:def metrics(record):
482:    # Reuse the campaign's receipt-time trapezoids; no extrapolation across missing samples.
483:    sys.path.insert(0,str(REPO/'benchmark/campaign'))
484:    import power
485:    samples = record['samples']
486:    start,end = record.get('measure_start'),record.get('measure_end')
487:    measured = [s for s in samples if start is not None and start <= s['t'] <= (end or start)]
488:    result = dict(peak_vmhwm_kib=max((s['vmhwm_kib'] for s in samples if s.get('vmhwm_kib') is not None),default=None),
489:                  peak_pss_kib=max((s['pss_kib'] for s in samples if s.get('pss_kib') is not None),default=None),
490:                  min_mem_available_kib=min((s['mem_available_kib'] for s in samples),default=None))
491:    if start is None or end is None:
492:        return result
493:    result['power'] = power.weighted(samples,start,end)
494:    result['skin_start'] = measured[0]['skin'] if measured else None
495:    result['skin_end'] = measured[-1]['skin'] if measured else None
496:    result['status_max'] = max((s['status'] for s in measured if s.get('status') is not None),default=None)
497:    # Marked copy from power_map.capped_by_policy: later sample owns the previous gap.
498:    result['caps'] = {}
499:    for policy in POLICIES:
500:        previous,capped,known = start,0.,0.
501:        for s in measured:
502:            dt = s['t']-previous
503:            previous = s['t']
504:            value,top = s['max'].get(policy),s['cpuinfo_max_khz'].get(policy)
505:            if value is not None and top is not None:
506:                known += dt
507:                if value < top:
508:                    capped += dt
509:        result['caps'][policy] = dict(percent=100*capped/(end-start),unknown_s=(end-start)-known)
510:    return result
513:def lmk_request(bridge, epoch):
514:    write(bridge/'lmk_request.json',dict(epoch=epoch))
515:    start = time.monotonic()
516:    while not (bridge/'lmk_done.json').exists():
517:        if time.monotonic()-start > 40:
518:            return dict(ok=False,error='LMK bridge timeout')
519:        time.sleep(.1)
520:    d = json.loads((bridge/'lmk_done.json').read_text())
521:    (bridge/'lmk_request.json').unlink()
522:    (bridge/'lmk_done.json').unlink()
523:    return d
526:def root(script, tag):
527:    """#123 form, native only: Android-service output goes to a file before it is read."""
528:    dest = f'/data/local/tmp/vis1_{os.getpid()}_{tag}.txt'
529:    p = subprocess.run(['su','-c',f'({script}) </dev/null >{dest} 2>&1'],
530:                       capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=30)
531:    q = subprocess.run(['su','-c',f'cat {dest}'],capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=10)
532:    if p.returncode or q.returncode:
533:        raise RuntimeError(f'root {tag} failed ({p.returncode}/{q.returncode})')
534:    return q.stdout
537:def parse_dump(text):
538:    # Marked copy from coresidency.parse_dump: HAL current section only, never cached temperatures.
539:    status = re.search(r'^Thermal Status: (\d+)\s*$',text,re.M)
540:    section = re.search(r'^Current temperatures from HAL:\n((?:[ \t]+[^\n]*\n?)*)',text,re.M)
541:    skin = re.search(r'Temperature\{mValue=([^,]+), mType=-?\d+, mName=VIRTUAL-SKIN,',
542:                     section.group(1) if section else '')
543:    return dict(status=int(status.group(1)) if status else None,skin=float(skin.group(1)) if skin else None)
546:def monitor(bridge):
547:    if Path('/termux-home').is_dir() or os.environ.get('PREFIX') != '/data/data/com.termux/files/usr':
548:        raise SystemExit('monitor must run in native Termux')
549:    os.sched_setaffinity(0,{0,1,2,3})
550:    slow_state, ready = {}, threading.Event()
551:    def slow_samples():
552:        while not (bridge/'stop').exists():
553:            began = time.monotonic()
554:            try:
555:                matches = agent_matches()
556:                slow_state['latest'] = dict(parse_dump(root('dumpsys thermalservice','thermal')),
557:                                            thermal_at=time.monotonic(),agent=bool(matches),agent_detail=matches)
558:            except Exception as e:
559:                slow_state['latest'] = dict(error=str(e))
560:                append(bridge/'errors.jsonl',dict(t=time.monotonic(),error=str(e)))
561:            ready.set()
562:            # Slow HAL calls must not create holes in campaign-style power integration.
563:            for _ in range(max(0,int((5-(time.monotonic()-began))/.1))):
564:                if (bridge/'stop').exists():return
565:                time.sleep(.1)
566:    slow_thread = threading.Thread(target=slow_samples,daemon=True)
567:    slow_thread.start()
568:    if not ready.wait(35):
569:        raise RuntimeError('native thermal monitor did not initialize')
570:    with (bridge/'samples.jsonl').open('a') as log:
571:        while not (bridge/'stop').exists():
572:            began = time.monotonic()
573:            try:
574:                latest = dict(slow_state['latest'])
575:                if latest.get('error'):
576:                    raise RuntimeError(latest['error'])
577:                pid = json.loads((bridge/'target.json').read_text()).get('pid') if (bridge/'target.json').exists() else None
578:                script = f'cat {BAT}/current_now {BAT}/voltage_now {BAT}/status {BAT}/capacity {BAT}/temp; '
579:                script += 'echo CAPS; '
580:                script += '; '.join(f'cat {CPU}/{p}/scaling_max_freq {CPU}/{p}/cpuinfo_max_freq' for p in POLICIES)
581:                script += '; echo CPU; cat /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone9/temp /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone10/temp /sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone11/temp'
582:                if pid:
583:                    script += f'; echo MEMORY; cat /proc/{pid}/status; echo PSS; cat /proc/{pid}/smaps_rollup'
584:                text = root(script,'sample')
585:                head, text = text.split('CAPS\n',1)
586:                current,voltage,status,capacity,bat_c = head.split()
587:                caps,text = text.split('CPU\n',1)
588:                nums = list(map(int,caps.split()))
589:                if len(nums) != 6 or int(voltage) <= 0:
590:                    raise RuntimeError('invalid power/cap fields')
591:                cpu = text.split('MEMORY\n')[0].split()
592:                row = dict(latest,t_start=began,t=time.monotonic(),current_now_uA=int(current),voltage_now_uV=int(voltage),
593:                           battery_w=-int(current)*int(voltage)/1e12,battery_status=status,capacity=int(capacity),bat_c=int(bat_c)/10,
594:                           max=dict(zip(POLICIES,nums[::2])),cpuinfo_max_khz=dict(zip(POLICIES,nums[1::2])),
595:                           cpu_mc=list(map(int,cpu[:3])),pid=pid)
596:                mem = Path('/proc/meminfo').read_text()
597:                row['mem_available_kib'] = int(re.search(r'MemAvailable:\s*(\d+)',mem).group(1))
598:                if pid:
599:                    for key,pat in [('vmhwm_kib',r'^VmHWM:\s*(\d+)'),('pss_kib',r'^Pss:\s*(\d+)')]:
600:                        m = re.search(pat,text,re.M)
601:                        row[key] = int(m.group(1)) if m else None
602:                    masks = re.findall(r'^Cpus_allowed_list:\s*(.+)$',text,re.M)
603:                    row['cores_ok'] = masks == ['4-7']
604:                if time.monotonic()-row['thermal_at'] > 12:
605:                    raise RuntimeError('thermal sample stale')
606:                write(bridge/'latest.json',row)
607:                log.write(json.dumps(row)+'\n');log.flush()
608:                if (bridge/'lmk_request.json').exists() and not (bridge/'lmk_done.json').exists():
609:                    epoch = float(json.loads((bridge/'lmk_request.json').read_text())['epoch'])
610:                    # Marked copy: coresidency.lmk_lines patterns, with no raw logcat retained (privacy).
611:                    # Filter before root output hits disk: never save unrelated Android logs or identities.
612:                    awk = "BEGIN {n=0;k=0} {s=tolower($0)} s ~ /lowmemorykiller|lmkd|killinfo/ {n++; if (s ~ /(lowmemorykiller|lmkd).*kill|killinfo/) k++} END {print n,k}"
613:                    counts = root(f"set -o pipefail; logcat -d -b all -v epoch -T {epoch:.3f} | awk '{awk}'",'lmk')
614:                    n,k = map(int,counts.split())
615:                    write(bridge/'lmk_done.json',dict(ok=True,n_lines=n,n_kills=k))
616:            except Exception as e:
617:                write(bridge/'latest.json',dict(t=time.monotonic(),error=str(e)))
618:                append(bridge/'errors.jsonl',dict(t=time.monotonic(),error=str(e)))
619:            # Campaign de-phased receipt-time power sampling, same 0.37 s period, no catch-up bursts.
620:            time.sleep(max(0,.37-(time.monotonic()-began)))
623:def manifest(selected, t2_draft, texts, images):
624:    paths = ['chat.py','server_manager.py','benchmark/llm_objective_setting/bench.py',
625:             'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis0/vis0.py','benchmark/campaign/power.py',

629:                commands={b:command(b,t2_draft) for b in selected},

631:                code_sha256={p:sha(REPO/p) for p in paths})
634:def completed(out):
635:    return {d['block'] for p in out.glob('[TV][1-4]_*.json')
636:            if (d:=json.loads(p.read_text())).get('label') == 'VALID'}
639:def preflight():
640:    """Everything oneshot.sh needs true before it idles 5 minutes: ports, files/hashes, agent, plus
641:    whichever of (Debian imports) / (root, battery, cpuset) matches where this process is running."""
642:    free_ports()
643:    matches = agent_matches()
644:    if matches:
645:        log_agent_matches(matches)
646:        raise SystemExit('agent found; close Claude/AGY/Codex (see pid/comm/cmdline above)')
647:    missing = [str(p) for p in (*EXPECTED_HASHES, *BINS.values()) if not p.is_file()]
648:    if missing:
649:        raise SystemExit('missing file(s), fetch/build first: ' + ', '.join(missing))
650:    bad = [str(p) for p, h in EXPECTED_HASHES.items() if sha(p) != h]
651:    if bad:
652:        raise SystemExit('SHA-256 mismatch, re-fetch/rebuild: ' + ', '.join(bad))
653:    if Path('/termux-home').is_dir():
654:        missing_mods = [m for m in DEBIAN_IMPORTS if importlib.util.find_spec(m) is None]
655:        if missing_mods:
656:            raise SystemExit('Debian python3 is missing ' + ', '.join(missing_mods) +
657:                              ' — run: apt-get install python3-requests python3-pil')
658:    else:
659:        if subprocess.run(['su', '-c', 'id'], capture_output=True, timeout=10).returncode != 0:
660:            raise SystemExit('no root (su) access; telemetry needs root')
661:        cap, status = root('cat /sys/class/power_supply/battery/capacity'
662:                            ' /sys/class/power_supply/battery/status', 'preflight_battery').split()[:2]
663:        if not (cap.isdigit() and 50 <= int(cap) <= 80 and status == 'Discharging'):
664:            raise SystemExit('battery outside 50-80% Discharging band; unplug and bring it into range')
665:        before = os.sched_getaffinity(0)
666:        os.sched_setaffinity(0, {4, 5, 6, 7})
667:        stuck = os.sched_getaffinity(0) == {4, 5, 6, 7}
668:        os.sched_setaffinity(0, before)
669:        if not stuck:
670:            raise SystemExit('Termux cpuset blocks cores 4-7; bring Termux to the foreground, screen on')
673:def smoke_block(block, out, bridge, t2_draft, allow_agent, payloads):
674:    """--smoke: one request per block, own server up and down, no 180 s loop or gate wait beyond
675:    the single telemetry reading already needed to launch. Always labelled SMOKE — NOT VALID TIMING."""
676:    record = dict(block=block, label=SMOKE_LABEL, command=command(block, t2_draft),
677:                  started_epoch=time.time(), server_started=False, reply_received=False,
678:                  exit_clean=None, image_tokens=None, reason=None, allow_agent=allow_agent)
679:    attempt = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S%fZ')
680:    logpath = out / f'{block}_{attempt}.server.log'
681:    proc = pid = None
682:    try:
683:        record['reading'] = telemetry(bridge, allow_agent=allow_agent)
684:        free_ports()
685:        if not allow_agent:
686:            matches = agent_matches()
687:            if matches:
688:                log_agent_matches(matches)
689:                raise RuntimeError('agent found before launch')
690:        with logpath.open('w') as log:
691:            began = time.monotonic()
692:            proc = spawn(record['command'], log)
693:            pid = None
694:            while pid is None:
695:                if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic() - began > 10:
696:                    raise RuntimeError('server crashed or owned child not found')
697:                try:
698:                    pid = server_pid(proc)
699:                except (OSError, RuntimeError):
700:                    time.sleep(.05)
701:            record['server_started'] = True
702:            write(bridge / 'target.json', dict(pid=pid))
703:            while True:
704:                if proc.poll() is not None:
705:                    raise RuntimeError('server crashed during load')
706:                try:
707:                    if post('/health', timeout=1).get('status') == 'ok':
708:                        break
709:                except (OSError, ValueError):
710:                    pass
711:                if time.monotonic() - began >= 180:
712:                    raise RuntimeError('server load timeout')
713:                time.sleep(.2)
714:            check_cores(pid)
715:            endpoint = '/completion' if block.startswith('T') else '/v1/chat/completions'
716:            name, body = payloads[0]
717:            offset = logpath.stat().st_size
718:            t0 = time.monotonic()
719:            data = post(endpoint, body, timeout=180)  # same bound as the real warm-up; V4 (BF16) took 55-72 s
720:            end = time.monotonic()
721:            with logpath.open() as f:
722:                f.seek(offset)
723:                delta = f.read()
724:            row = request_record(name, data, t0, end, delta, block)
725:            record['reply_received'] = True
726:            record['image_tokens'] = row.get('image_tokens')
727:            record['requests'] = [row]
728:    except Exception as e:
729:        record['reason'] = str(e)
730:    finally:
731:        write(bridge / 'target.json', dict(pid=None))
732:        if proc is not None:
733:            # One TERM to the server lets it exit 0; clean = exit 0 and nothing left in our group.
734:            record['server_exit'] = own_stop(proc, pid)
735:            record['exit_clean'] = record['server_exit'] == 0 and not group_alive(proc.pid)
736:        write(out / f'{block}_{attempt}.json', record)
737:    return record
740:def main(argv=None):
741:    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
742:    ap.add_argument('--only')
743:    ap.add_argument('--resume',type=Path)
744:    ap.add_argument('--dry-run',action='store_true')
745:    ap.add_argument('--bridge',type=Path)
746:    ap.add_argument('--preflight',action='store_true',help=argparse.SUPPRESS)

20:@contextlib.contextmanager
21:def patched(*triples):
22:    saved = [(obj, name, getattr(obj, name)) for obj, name, _ in triples]
23:    try:
24:        for obj, name, value in triples:
25:            setattr(obj, name, value)
26:        yield
27:    finally:
28:        for obj, name, value in saved:
29:            setattr(obj, name, value)

38:                 (sm, 'wait_for_server', lambda port=8080, timeout=60: True), (sm, 'mtp_draft_ok', lambda: True)):

40:            sm.start_setup(setup)

codex
Found five P2 issues that should be addressed before timed owner runs. No P0 or P1 finding. Review remained read-only; no servers, benchmarks, motors, or other reviewers were launched.

Reviewed candidate and shared harness line counts:

| File | Lines |
|---|---:|
| `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py` | 530 |
| `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/test_vis3.py` | 288 |
| `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/oneshot.sh` | 110 |
| `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md` | 75 |
| `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis2/vis2_run.py` | 489 |
| `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis1/vis1_run.py` | 838 |
| `review_inputs/DIAGNOSIS.md` | 16 |

Below, `vis3_run.py` means `benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/vis3_run.py`.

1. **P2 — Effective runtime settings can differ while pooled runs pass comparability.**

   `vis3_run.py:337`:
   ```python
        if os.environ.get('LD_LIBRARY_PATH'):
   ```

   `vis3_run.py:403`:
   ```python
    keys = ('code_sha256', 'payload_sha256', 'weight_sha256', 'binary_sha256', 'rounds', 'first_n', 'skin_start_max')
   ```

   Preflight checks library search paths, but neither rejects nor records llama/OpenMP environment overrides. Batch size, ubatch size, flash attention, and loading mode remain implicit. Both upstream argument parsers accept environment overrides for these settings.

   Concrete scenario: separately launched O and N folders use different container environment settings for `LLAMA_ARG_UBATCH`. Their manifests still compare equal, and identical token counts do not reveal the different prompt-processing configuration. A legacy mmap environment override can also affect OLD while NEW has removed that option.

   Reject relevant overrides or explicitly control and record effective settings; include them in pooling checks. Add a regression covering different effective configurations.

2. **P2 — Warm-phase throughput can be thermally confounded without the summary exposing its starting conditions.**

   `vis3_run.py:237`:
   ```python
        for name in ('cold', 'warm'):  # warm = immediate relaunch, files still in the page cache
   ```

   `vis3_run.py:383`:
   ```python
        gate_skin=(d.get('gate') or {}).get('skin'), phases=phase,
   ```

   The strengthened gate runs before the cold phase. Cold loading, initialization, and the first completion then heat the phone before the immediate warm launch. The summary’s start-skin comparison uses the earlier gate reading, rather than either phase’s actual launch temperature.

   Concrete scenario: OLD and NEW both pass at 31 °C, but their cold initialization leaves different temperatures or frequency caps. Warm throughput then differs partly because of that carry-over, while reported start-skin spread is zero.

   Gate the warm launch without dropping caches, and assess actual per-phase starting temperatures/caps. The existing CRITICAL and battery stops remain intact; this finding concerns comparison validity.

3. **P2 — A failed mapped-library check does not reject the block.**

   `vis3_run.py:248`:
   ```python
            phase['libs'] = mapped_libs(pid, build)
   ```

   `vis3_run.py:274`:
   ```python
        record.update(label=SMOKE_LABEL if smoke else 'VALID', reason='completed cold + warm sequence')
   ```

   A false `libs['ok']` is only reported later by summary checks. Execution continues through the measurement sequence and can receive `VALID`; aggregate configuration medians and NEW/OLD ratios still include it.

   Concrete scenario: a launcher maps a ggml/llama library from another build directory. The harness detects the mismatch but continues measuring a configuration outside the intended OLD/NEW comparison.

   Raise immediately on a failed library check, preserving the evidence in the failed record. Add a lifecycle test with `mapped_libs(...).ok == False`.

4. **P2 — The added intermediate shutdown races with the native memory sampler.**

   `vis3_run.py:198`:
   ```python
    v1.write(bridge/'target.json', dict(pid=None))
   ```

   `vis3_run.py:289`:
   ```python
            if [e for e in v1.rows(bridge/'errors.jsonl') if record['phase_start'] <= e['t'] <= record['stop_start']]:
   ```

   Clearing `target.json` does not acknowledge that an already-running monitor sample has finished. That sample may still hold the cold server PID when shutdown removes its `/proc` files. The reused monitor then records a sampling failure.

   Unlike VIS-2’s final shutdown, this cold-phase shutdown occurs inside the interval checked for sampling errors. A normal, clean exit can therefore invalidate the completed block, or transient error telemetry can abort the warm launch.

   Synchronize sampler detachment before stopping the server. Keep sampling failures during live measurement fatal. Add a test covering an in-flight sample during cold-to-warm shutdown.

5. **P2 — Preflight can declare readiness with a required Python dependency missing.**

   `vis3_run.py:351`:
   ```python
    print('PREFLIGHT OK', flush=True)
   ```

   `chat.py:1`:
   ```python
import sys, os, requests, time, subprocess, shutil
   ```

   VIS-3 removed the Debian dependency check inherited by VIS-2, but payload construction still reaches `chat.py` through `v1.text_payload()`.

   Concrete scenario: Debian lacks `requests`. Both wrapper preflights pass and readiness is announced; the owner subsequently waits through the five-minute idle before payload construction fails.

   Check the dependencies actually needed by this text-only harness before reporting readiness. Pillow need not remain required. Add a missing-`requests` preflight test.

The diagnosis is broadly supported for the listed compiler/CPU build settings, draft-thread inheritance, disabled per-request speculative adjustments, buffered projector reads, combined teardown, and disabled subprocess support. However, “identical logic” overstates the speculative conversion comparison: NEW contains additional behavior, although its thread-inheritance branch matches OLD. The archived help also distinguishes OLD’s `mmap` default from NEW’s `auto`; equivalent behavior on this CPU should be described separately from identical defaults.

The upstream reload PR was closed on August 11, and the projector/draft eviction proposal remains a draft aimed at GPU memory swapping. [Reload PR](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/25963), [eviction PR](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/28346). I could not verify issue #30776, independently recount the instruction totals, or fully reproduce the historical thermal figures.

The supplied outputs report VIS-3 13/13 and VIS-2 passing, plus the disclosed VIS-1 wrapper-contract error. My permitted VIS-3 rerun exited 182 without output, so those results were inspected but not independently reproduced. Current tests cover command parity and the happy lifecycle, but miss the failure scenarios above. The wrapper adaptation preserves the existing thermal stops and owned-process handling; I found no deliberate unrelated-process termination path.
